[^43a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 44

**Ruling**[^44-1]

The FOEN decides by issuing a ruling on the participation of installation operators in the ETS and regarding installations not taken into account in accordance with Article 43.

[^44-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 45

**Maximum available quantity of emission allowances**[^45-1]

<sup>1</sup> The FOEN shall calculate the available quantity of emission allowances each year for all installation operators in the ETS as a whole. The calculation is made in accordance with Annex 8.

<sup>2</sup>

It shall each year retain a portion of the quantity calculated pursuant to paragraph 1 in order to make it accessible to the following installation operators:

- a.[^45-2] installation operators that are entitled to the allocation of emission allowances free of charge pursuant to Article 46*a* paragraph 1; and
- b.

  installation operators that are already participating in the ETS, provided:

  - 1\. they bring additional sub-installations under Article 46*a* paragraph 2 into operation, or
  - 2\. the quantity of emission allowances to be allocated to them free of charge on the basis of Article 46*b* is increased.

<sup>3</sup>

The portion under paragraph 2 is the sum of:

- a.[^45-3] at least 5 per cent of the emission allowances under paragraph 1; and
- b.

  all the emission allowances that are no longer allocated free of charge based on:

  - 1\. the exemption from the requirement to participate in the ETS under Article 41 or as a result of withdrawals from the ETS under Article 43*a*,
  - 1<sup>bis</sup>.[^45-4] a reduction under Article 46 paragraph 1<sup>bis</sup>,
  - 2\. adjustments under Article 46*b*,
  - 3\. an incorrect or incomplete monitoring report (Art. 52 para. 8).

<sup>4</sup>

If the portion under paragraph 2 is insufficient to meet claims in full, the emission allowances shall be allocated in the following order:

- a. operators under Article 46*a* that have been participating in the ETS for at least one full calendar year or that have been operating their new sub-installations for at least one full calendar year;
- b. operators under Article 46*a* that began participating in the ETS in the previous year or that brought their new sub-installations into operation in the previous year;
- c. installation operators under paragraph 2 letter b number 2;
- d. installation operators under Article 46*a* that are participating in the ETS for the first time in the year concerned that brought their new sub-installations into operation in the year concerned.[^45-5]

<sup>5</sup> If the claims cannot be met in full within a group under paragraph 4 letter a, b or d, the time that participation in the ETS began or when the new sub-installations came into operation determines how the emission allowances are allocated to the individual operators. If notification is made after starting the activity or after the new sub-installation comes into operation, the date of notification applies.[^45-6]

<sup>6</sup> If the claims cannot be met in full within the group under paragraph 4 letter c, the FOEN shall reduce the quantity of emission allowances allocated to the individual operators free of charge on a pro rata basis.[^45-7]

[^45-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^45-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^45-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^45-4]: Inserted by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).
[^45-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^45-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^45-7]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 46

**Allocating emission allowances free of charge**[^46-1]

<sup>1</sup> The FOEN calculates the quantity of emission allowances to be allocated free of charge annually to installation operators, based on the benchmarks and adjustment factors described in Annex 9 and taking account of European Union regulations.

<sup>1bis</sup> If the target agreement under Article 46 EnA[^46-2] is not met, the calculated amount shall be reduced by 20 per cent. This does not apply to operators of installations that have a roadmap in accordance with Article 5 of the Federal Act of 30 September 2022[^46-3] on Climate Protection Goals, Innovation and Strengthening Energy Security (CIA) and have implemented the measures set out therein in the preceding years.[^46-4]

<sup>2</sup> The proportionate reduction under Article 19 paragraph 7 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act is calculated in advance for the allocation periods in accordance with Annex 9 number 2.3. The proportionate reduction is limited to up to 5 per cent annually.[^46-5]

[^46-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^46-2]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8495)
[^46-3]: [SR 814.310](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/40153)
[^46-4]: Inserted by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).
[^46-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 46a

**Emission allowances allocated free of charge to installation operators participating in the ETS for the first time and to installation operators with new sub-installations**[^46a-1]

<sup>1</sup> An installation operator that participates for the first time in the ETS from 2 January 2026 shall be allocated emission allowances free of charge from the reserve in accordance with Article 45 paragraph 2 from the start of its participation in the ETS.[^46a-2]

<sup>2</sup> If an operator already participating in the ETS brings into operation an additional unit affecting the emission allowances to be allocated free of charge (a sub-installation), it shall be allocated emission allowances free of charge from the portion mentioned in Article 45 paragraph 2 from the start of operations.

<sup>3</sup> Emission allowances are allocated free of charge in accordance with Article 46 and 46*b*.

[^46a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^46a-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

#### Art. 46b

**Adjustment of the quantity of emission allowances to be allocated free of charge**[^46b-1]

<sup>1</sup> The quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge annually to an installation operator is adjusted if the activity rate of a sub-installation is changed to the extent specified in Annex 9 number 5.1.1. The adjustment is made in accordance with the requirements in Annex 9 number 5.1.

<sup>2</sup> If the quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge is calculated on the basis of the heat benchmark (Annex 9 No 1.2) or fuel benchmark (Annex 9 No 1.3), the quantity shall only be adjusted if, in addition to any change under paragraph 1, the expected activity rate of a sub-installation is changed within the scope of Annex 9 number 5.1*a*.1. The adjustment is made in accordance with the requirements in Annex 9 number 5.1*a*.[^46b-2]

<sup>3</sup> …[^46b-3]

<sup>4</sup> The quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge annually to an operator shall also be adjusted if any parameter under Annex 9 number 5.2.3 changes to the extent specified in Annex 9 number 5.2.1. The adjustment is made in accordance with the requirements in Annex 9 number 5.2.

<sup>5</sup> If a sub-installation ceases to operate, the operator shall no longer be allocated emission allowances free of charge for this sub-installation from the time that operations cease.

<sup>6</sup> …[^46b-4]

[^46b-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^46b-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).
[^46b-3]: Repealed by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).
[^46b-4]: Inserted by No I of the O of 29 Sept. 2023 ([AS 2023 581](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/581)). Repealed by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

#### Art. 46c

[^46c-1]

[^46c-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Repealed by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, with effect from 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

### Section 1*a* Aircraft Operators

[^chap4-sec1a-1]

[^chap4-sec1a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 46d

**Aircraft operators with the obligation to participate**

<sup>1</sup> An aircraft operator is obliged to participate in the ETS in accordance with the Annex of the Ordinance on Air Navigation of 14 November 1973[^46d-1] (aircraft operators) if it operates flights in accordance with Annex 13.

<sup>2</sup> An aircraft operator required to participate in the ETS must register immediately with the competent authority in accordance with Annex 14.

<sup>3</sup> If the operator cannot be determined, the keeper and, at a subsidiary level, the owner of the aircraft is deemed the aircraft operator.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN may request an aircraft operator to designate an address for service in Switzerland.

[^46d-1]: [SR 748.01](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/9034)

#### Art. 46e

**Maximum quantity of emission allowances available**[^46e-1]

<sup>1</sup> The FOEN shall calculate the maximum quantity of emission allowances to be made available each year to all aircraft operators. The calculation is made in accordance with Annex 15 number 1.[^46e-2]

<sup>2</sup> If the geographical scope of the ETS changes, the FOEN may adjust the maximum quantity of emission allowances for aviation available annually and the quantity of emission allowances allocated to aircraft operators free of charge. In doing so, it shall take account of the corresponding EU rules.

<sup>3 and <sup>4</sup></sup> …[^46e-3]

[^46e-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^46e-2]: Amended by No I of the O 29 Sept. 2023, in force since 1 Jan. 2024 ([AS 2023 581](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/581)).
[^46e-3]: Repealed by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 46f

[^46f-1]

[^46f-1]: Repealed by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

#### Art. 46g

[^46g-1]

[^46g-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 29 Sept. 2023 ([AS 2023 581](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/581)). Repealed by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

#### Art. 46h

**Emission allowances to be allocated free of charge for the use of renewable and low-emission aviation fuels**[^46h-1]

<sup>1</sup> For the period 2026–2030, 550,000 emission allowances are available for free allocation for the use of renewable or low-emission aviation fuels as defined in Annex 15 number 5.1 on flights covered by the ETS.

<sup>2</sup> Commercial aircraft operators may apply by 31 March of each year for the free allocation of emission allowances for flights undertaken in the previous year.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN shall calculate the quantity of emission allowances available for allocation free of charge to aircraft operators in accordance with Annex 15 numbers 5.2–5.5.

<sup>4</sup> If the total quantity of emission allowances applied for under the free allocation scheme exceeds the available quantity, the FOEN shall reduce the quantity to be allocated to individual operators on a pro rata basis.

<sup>5</sup> The FOEN shall publish the quantities of emission allowances allocated free of charge to individual aircraft operators each year.

[^46h-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

### Section 2 Auction of Emission Allowances

[^chap4-sec2-1]

[^chap4-sec2-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 47

**Eligibility for admission**

Installation and aircraft operators participating in the Swiss and the EU emissions trading systems and companies from the European Economic Area (EEA) admitted to bid in auctions in the European Union are eligible for admission to the auction of emission allowances provided they have an account in accordance with Article 57.

#### Art. 48

**Conducting the auction**

<sup>1</sup> The FOEN shall regularly auction the emission allowances for installations and aircraft for the corresponding year that are not allocated free of charge.[^48-1]

<sup>1bis</sup> The quantity of emission allowances for installations to be auctioned shall be reduced by fifty per cent if the difference between the supply of emission allowances for installations and the demand for emission allowances for installations (quantity in circulation) is more than fifty percent of the maximum quantity of emission allowances for installations available in the previous year in accordance with Article 45 paragraph 1. The calculation of the quantity in circulation shall be carried out in accordance with the provisions of Annex 8 number 2.[^48-2]

<sup>2</sup>

The FOEN may cancel the auction without accepting a bid if:

- a. it suspects agreements affecting competition or unlawful practices by dominant participants in the auction;
- b. the clearing price in the auction period differs significantly from the relevant price on the secondary market in the European Union; or
- c. safety risks or other reasons jeopardise the proper implementation of the auction.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN must report any suspicions under paragraph 2 letter a to the competition authorities.

<sup>4</sup> If the auction is cancelled for any of the reasons in paragraph 2 or if demand for the quantity of emission allowances assigned to an auction is not fully met, the remaining emission allowances are transferred to a subsequent auction.

<sup>5</sup> The emission allowances which are not assigned to an auction are cancelled at the end of the obligation period.

<sup>6</sup> The FOEN may commission private organisations to conduct the auction.

[^48-1]: Amended by No I of the O 29 Sept. 2023, in force since 1 Jan. 2024 ([AS 2023 581](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/581)).
[^48-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 24 Nov. 2021 ([AS 2021 859](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2021/859)). Amended by No I of the O 29 Sept. 2023, in force since 1 Jan. 2024 ([AS 2023 581](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/581)).

#### Art. 49

**Information to be submitted for admission**

<sup>1</sup>

Installation and aircraft operators participating in the Swiss and the European Union emissions trading systems and the other companies from the EEA admitted to bid in auctions in the European Union which are participating in the auction of emission allowances must submit the following information to the FOEN beforehand:

- a. first names, surnames, postal address, personal email address, mobile telephone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one, but no more than four, authorised auction agents;
- b. first names, surnames, postal address, personal email address, mobile telephone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one, but no more than four, bid validators;
- c. declaration that they and the authorised auction agents and bid validators accept the general conditions of the auction.

<sup>2</sup> Persons under paragraph 1 are not required to submit a Swiss criminal record certificate if they provide proof by means of notarial certificate that they have not been convicted of any of the criminal offences set out in Article 59*a* paragraph 1 letter b.

<sup>3</sup> Installation and aircraft operators obliged to participate in the ETS in the European Union must provide evidence of an operator account in the EU register and designate an address for service in Switzerland in addition to paragraph 1.

<sup>4</sup>

The companies from the EEA admitted to bid in auctions in the European Union must designate an address for service in Switzerland and submit the following information in addition to paragraph 1:

- a. evidence of direct admission to bid in auctions in the European Union;
- b. information on the categorisation in accordance with European Union regulations;
- c. an affirmation that they are participating in the auction solely on their own account.

<sup>5</sup> The FOEN may request additional information if it requires it for the participation in the auction.

<sup>6</sup> The proof of identity and criminal record certificates under paragraph 1 letters a and b and the information under paragraph 5 must be authenticated. Copies of documents issued outside of Switzerland must be apostilled. The date of the documents to be submitted and the authentication or apostille may not be more than three months before the date of application.

<sup>7</sup> The information is recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry.

#### Art. 49a

**Binding nature of the auction bids**

<sup>1</sup> Bids for the auction of emission allowances are made in euro and are binding after a bid validator gives consent.

<sup>2</sup> The invoice for the auctioned emission allowances must be settled in euro and via a bank account in Switzerland or in the EEA. The FOEN may exclude participants from future auctions if they fail to settle the invoice.

### Section 3 Data Collection and Monitoring

#### Art. 50

**Data collection**[^50-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The FOEN or an FOEN-authorised entity shall collect the data concerning installation operators required for:

- a. calculating the maximum quantity of emission allowances available each year to all installation operators in the ETS;
- b. the initial calculation of the quantity of emission allowances to be allocated free of charge.[^50-2]

<sup>1bis</sup> The operator shall collect the data required to adjust the quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge under Article 46*b*.[^50-3]

<sup>2</sup> The installation operator is required to cooperate. If it violates its obligation to cooperate, then it shall be denied free emission allowances.

<sup>3</sup> Aircraft operators are responsible for collecting the data relating to their activities under this Ordinance.

[^50-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^50-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^50-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 51

**Monitoring plan**[^51-1]

<sup>1</sup> Installation operators participating shall submit a monitoring plan to the competent authority for approval in accordance with Annex 14 no later than three months after the deadline for notification under Article 40 paragraph 2 or after the submission of an application to participate under Article 42. They shall use the template provided or approved by the FOEN.[^51-2]

<sup>2</sup> Aircraft operators participating in the Swiss ETS shall submit a monitoring plan to the competent authority for approval in accordance with Annex 14 no later than three months after notification of the obligation to participate in accordance with Article 46*d* paragraph 2 for the first time. If the monitoring plan must be submitted to the FOEN, they use the template provided or approved by the FOEN.[^51-3]

<sup>3</sup> The monitoring plan must meet the requirements set out in Annex 16.

<sup>4</sup> The monitoring plan must be amended if it no longer meets the requirements of Annex 16. The amended monitoring plan must be submitted to the competent authority for approval in accordance with Annex 14.[^51-4]

<sup>5</sup> The CO<sub>2</sub> monitoring plan in accordance with the Ordinance of 2 June 2017[^51-5] on the acquisition of tonne-kilometre data and the drawing-up of monitoring plans for flight routes is deemed the monitoring plan.

[^51-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^51-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^51-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^51-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^51-5]: [[AS 2017 3477](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/378); [2019 1477](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/284)]

#### Art. 52

**Monitoring report**[^52-1]

<sup>1</sup> Installation operators and aircraft operators must submit a monitoring report to the competent authority in accordance with Annex 14 for each year by 31 March of the following year. If the monitoring report must be submitted to the FOEN, the template provided or approved by the FOEN must be used.[^52-2]

<sup>2</sup> The monitoring report must contain the relevant information in accordance with Annex 17. The FOEN may request additional information if required for monitoring.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN may require at any time that the monitoring report of installation operators be verified by an FOEN-approved verification body.

<sup>4</sup> Aircraft operators must have their monitoring report verified by a verification body in accordance with Annex 18.

<sup>5</sup> The monitoring report of aircraft operators with carbon emissions that fall below the thresholds set out in Article 28*a* paragraph 4 of Directive 2003/87/EC[^52-3] is deemed to be verified if the carbon emissions were measured using the instrument for small emitters in accordance with Regulation (EU) 606/2010[^52-4] and the data used are those of the European Organisation for Safety of Air Navigation (Eurocontrol).[^52-5]

<sup>6</sup> If a monitoring report contains errors, is incomplete or is not submitted by the deadline, the competent authority shall estimate the relevant emissions in accordance with Annex 14 at the expense of the installation or aircraft operator.[^52-6]

<sup>7</sup> If there are doubts about the correctness of the verified monitoring report, the competent authority may adjust the emissions at its own dutiful discretion in accordance with Annex 14.

<sup>8</sup> If the information in the monitoring report required for an adjustment under Article 46*b* is incorrect or incomplete, the FOEN shall fix an appropriate deadline for its rectification. If the monitoring report is not rectified by this deadline, no emission allowances shall be allocated free of charge to the sub-installations concerned for the year concerned.[^52-7]

[^52-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^52-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^52-3]: Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 13 October 2003 establishing a system for greenhouse gas emission allowance trading within the Community and amending Council Directive 96/61/EC, OJ L 275 of 25.10.2003, p. 32; last amended by Regulation (EU) 2024/795, 29.2.2024.
[^52-4]: Commission Regulation (EU) No 606/2010 of 9 July 2010 on the approval of a simplified tool developed by the European organisation for air safety navigation (Eurocontrol) to estimate the fuel consumption of certain small emitting aircraft operators, last amended by OJ L 175 of 10.7.2010, p. 25.
[^52-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^52-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^52-7]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 53

**Obligation to report changes**[^53-1]

<sup>1</sup>

ETS participants shall inform the competent authority without delay in accordance with Annex 14 about:

- a. changes that could affect the emission allowances to be allocated free of charge;
- b. changes in contact information.

<sup>2</sup> Aircraft operators which no longer perform flights in accordance with Annex 13 must report this to the competent authority under Annex 14 no later than three months after cessation of the relevant flight activities.

<sup>3</sup>

Installation operators that carry on an activity in accordance with Annex 6 and that are exempt from the requirement to participate in the ETS shall notify the FOEN immediately:

- a. if the annual greenhouse gas emissions from the installations amount to 25 000 tonnes of CO<sub>2</sub>eq or more;
- b. of any changes to their contact details.[^53-2]

[^53-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^53-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 54

**Tasks of the cantons**

<sup>1</sup> The cantons shall verify whether installation operators have met their information obligations under Article 40 paragraph 2 and Article 53 paragraph 1 and 3 and whether the information provided is complete and traceable.[^54-1]

<sup>2</sup> The FOEN makes the required information available to the cantons.

<sup>3</sup> If a canton determines that the requirements of this Ordinance have not been met, it informs the FOEN without delay.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN may consult the cantons in order to answer questions if this is necessary in order to implement the provisions on the ETS.[^54-2]

[^54-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^54-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

### Section 4 Obligation to Surrender Emission Allowances

[^chap4-sec4-1]

[^chap4-sec4-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 55

**Obligation**[^55-1]

<sup>1</sup> Each year installation operators shall surrender to the FOEN emission allowances based on the relevant greenhouse gas emissions of the installations that have been taken into account.[^55-2]

<sup>1bis</sup>

CO<sub>2</sub> shall not be considered a relevant greenhouse gas emission if it:

- a. is emitted and permanently stored geologically in Switzerland or that is chemically bonded with mineral carbonates used in construction products in accordance with the requirements of Annex 19;
- b. is emitted and permanently stored in a contracting party to the EEA at a geological storage site within the meaning of Chapter 3 of Directive 2009/31/EC[^55-3] or chemically bonded with mineral carbonates used in construction products.[^55-4]

<sup>2</sup> Each year aircraft operators shall surrender emission allowances to the competent authority under Annex 14 based on the aircraft operator’s carbon emissions as recorded in accordance with Article 52.[^55-5]

<sup>2bis</sup> If an aircraft operator has to fulfil obligations under both the Swiss ETS and the EU ETS, the FOEN shall first count the emission allowances surrendered by the operators it manages towards fulfilling the obligation under the EU ETS.[^55-6]

<sup>3</sup> ETS participants shall by 30 September of each year meet the obligation for the greenhouse gas emissions of the previous year.[^55-7]

[^55-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^55-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^55-3]: Directive 2009/31/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 23 April 2009 on the geological storage of carbon dioxide and amending Council Directive 85/337/EEC and European Parliament and Council Directives 2000/60/EC, 2001/80/EC, 2004/35/EC, 2006/12/EC and 2008/1/EC and Regulation (EC) No 1013/2006, OJ L 140 of 5.6.2009, p. 114; last amended by Regulation (EU) 2018/1999, OJ L 328 of 21.12.2018, p. 1.
[^55-4]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^55-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^55-6]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^55-7]: Amended by No I of the O 29 Sept. 2023, in force since 1 Jan. 2024 ([AS 2023 581](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2023/581)).

#### Art. 55a

**Case of hardship**[^55a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

On application, the FOEN may, in cases where European emission allowances are not recognised in the Swiss ETS in accordance with Article 4 paragraph 1 of the ETS Agreement[^55a-2], count European emission allowances towards meeting the obligation of an ETS participant in accordance with Article 55 if the ETS participant proves that:

- a. it cannot meet its surrender obligation in accordance with Article 55 unless these allowances are credited;
- b. it has participated in an auction of emission allowances in accordance with Article 48 and thus has made offers for the required quantity of emission allowances at market prices;
- c. procuring the lacking emission allowances issued by the Confederation in accordance with Article 45 paragraph 1 or Article 46*e* paragraph 1 outside auctions would significantly impair the ETS participant's competitiveness.

<sup>2</sup> To assess significant impairment to competitiveness, the FOEN shall also takes account in particular of the ETS participant's receipts from the sale of emission allowances issued by the Confederation.

<sup>3</sup> The application must be submitted to the FOEN no later than 31 March of the following year the year for which the case of hardship is claimed for the first time. The FOEN decides annually on the quantity of eligible European emission allowances.

<sup>4</sup> If no linking with the European Emissions Trading Registry exists or is likely to exist in the foreseeable future, the European emission allowances must be transferred annually to a Swiss Confederation account in the European Union Emissions Trading Registry.

[^55a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^55a-2]: [SR 0.814.011.268](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/3477)

#### Art. 55b–55d

[^55b55d-1]

[^55b55d-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)). Repealed by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, with effect from 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 56

**Non-compliance with obligations**

<sup>1</sup> If an ETS participant does not meet its obligations to surrender emission allowances by the deadline, then the FOEN shall impose a penalty in accordance with Article 21 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.[^56-1]

<sup>2</sup> The payment deadline is 30 days from the issue of the ruling. If a payment is late, default interest is charged. The FDF shall fix the rate of interest.[^56-2]

<sup>3</sup> If an ETS participant does not surrender emission allowances by 31 January of the following year, then they shall be offset against the emission allowances allocated to the installation operator free of charge for that year.[^56-3]

[^56-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^56-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^56-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

### Section 5 Emissions Trading Registry

[^chap4-sec5-1]

[^chap4-sec5-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).

#### Art. 57

**Principles**[^57-1]

<sup>1</sup> ETS participants must have an operator account in the Emissions Trading Registry; aircraft operators under the administration of a foreign authority in accordance with Annex 14 are exempted.

<sup>2</sup> Installation and aircraft operators participating in the European Union ETS and the other companies from the EEA admitted to bid in auctions in the European Union that wish to take part in the auction must have a personal account.

<sup>3</sup> Importers and manufacturers of fossil motor fuels in accordance with Chapter 7 that hold emission allowances, emission-reduction certifications or attestations in the Emissions Trading Registry, or that want to trade them, must have an operator or a personal account.[^57-2]

<sup>4</sup> All other companies and persons that hold emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations in the Emissions Trading Registry, or that want to trade them, must have a personal account.

<sup>5</sup> Companies, operators and persons that receive attestations for a project or a programme in accordance with Article 5, for emission reductions in accordance with Article 12, or for emission reductions arising from a target agreement with an emissions target in accordance with Article 12*a* may have them issued directly to a third party’s operator or personal account.

<sup>6</sup> A personal account holder may keep not more than a million emission allowances in their personal accounts.

[^57-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^57-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 58

**Opening an account**[^58-1]

<sup>1</sup> An application must be made to the FOEN to open an account in accordance with Article 57.

<sup>2</sup>

The application must include:

- a. for installation or aircraft operators and other companies: an extract from the commercial register and a copy of the proof of identity of the person authorised to represent the operator or company;
- b. for natural persons: proof of identity;
- b<sup>bis</sup>.[^58-2] for competent authorities of a partner state: official confirmation from the government and a copy of the proof of identity of the person authorised to represent the operator or company;
- c. first names, surnames, postal and email addresses and proof of identity of the applicant;
- d. first names, surnames, postal address, personal email address, mobile phone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one and no more than four authorised representatives for the account;
- e. first names, surnames, postal address, personal email address, mobile phone number, proof of identity and criminal record certificate of at least one and no more than four transaction validators;
- f. declaration that the applicant accepts the General Terms and Conditions of the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>3</sup> The submission of a Swiss criminal record certificate is not required if evidence can be provided by means of notarial certificate that the persons concerned have not been convicted of the criminal offences set out in Article 59*a* paragraph 1 letter b.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN may request additional information if required to open the account.

<sup>5</sup> Companies registered in a state in which no commercial register is maintained shall confirm by another form of supporting document their existence and the authorisation to sign of the person entitled to represent the company.

<sup>6</sup> Information on commercial register extracts, proof of identity, criminal record certificates and information in accordance with paragraphs 4 and 5 must be authenticated. Copies of documents issued outside of Switzerland must be apostilled. The date of the documents to be submitted and the authentication or apostille may not be more than three months before the application date.

<sup>7</sup> The FOEN opens the requested account after reviewing the information and documents and as soon as the applicant has paid the fee.

<sup>8</sup> Aircraft operators administered by the FOEN must submit an application to open an account in the Emissions Trading Registry within 30 working days after the approval of the aircraft operator’s monitoring plan or its transferral to Switzerland. The application must contain the unique aircraft code(s) of the aircraft operated by the applicant which fall under the Swiss ETS or the European Union ETS.

[^58-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^58-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 59

**Address for service and registered office or domicile**[^59-1]

<sup>1</sup>

Any company or person with a personal account under Article 57 must designate an address for service in Switzerland for the following persons:

- a. for companies, the person entitled to represent the company, or for natural persons, the account holder;
- b. the authorised representatives for the account; and
- c. the transaction validators.

<sup>2</sup>

Any operator or person with an operator or personal account under Article 57 must designate an address for service in Switzerland or in the EEA for the following persons:

- a. the auction agents; and
- b. the bid validators.

<sup>2bis</sup> Any person or company resident or based in the United Kingdom may designate an address for service in the United Kingdom for persons under paragraph 2 instead of an address for service in Switzerland or in the EEA.[^59-2]

<sup>3</sup> A company that has an operator account or personal account in accordance with Article 57 must designate a registered office in Switzerland or in the EEA and hold a bank account in Switzerland or in the EEA.

<sup>4</sup> For an operator account or personal account of persons under Article 57, the account holder must designate a place of domicile in Switzerland or in the EEA and hold a bank account in Switzerland or in the EEA.

<sup>5</sup>

Paragraphs 3 and 4 do not apply:

- a. to accounts of aircraft operators outside of Switzerland and the EEA;
- b. to companies and persons based or registered in the United Kingdom, provided that they hold a bank account in Switzerland, the EEA or the United Kingdom;
- c.[^59-3] to the competent authorities of a partner state.[^59-4]

[^59-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^59-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).
[^59-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^59-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 59a

**Refusal to open an account**[^59a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The FOEN shall refuse to open an account or record authorised representatives for the account, authorised auction agents, transaction validators and bid validators if:

- a. the transmitted information or documents are incomplete, incorrect or not traceable;
- b. the company, the managing director or one of the persons mentioned in the introductory sentence has been convicted in the previous ten years of money laundering or criminal offences against property or of other criminal offences in connection with emissions trading or the legislation on financial market infrastructures, the financing of terrorism or other serious offences where the account was misused.

<sup>2</sup> It shall suspend the account opening or entry if an investigation is pending against the company or a person mentioned in paragraph 1 letter b due to any of the criminal offences mentioned in paragraph 1 letter b.

<sup>3</sup> If the FOEN refuses to open an account for an installation or aircraft operator that is obliged to participate in the ETS, then the FOEN shall open a frozen account to which the emission allowances allocated under Article 46, 46*b* or 46*f* are credited. The account shall remain frozen until the reasons that led to the refusal to open an account no longer apply.[^59a-2]

[^59a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)). Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^59a-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).

#### Art. 60

**Entry in the Emissions Trading Registry**[^60-1]

<sup>1</sup> All emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates, attestations and auction bids must be recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>2</sup> Changes in the holding of emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates and attestations are valid only if they are recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>3</sup>

Emission-reduction certificates for the following emission reductions may not be recorded in the Emissions Trading Registry:

- a. long-term certified emission reductions (lCER);
- b. temporary certified emission reductions (tCER);
- c. certified emission reductions from projects for carbon capture and geological carbon sequestration (CCS).

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN maintains a record of issuing attestations and emission allowances in the form of an electronic database.[^60-2]

[^60-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^60-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 25 Nov. 2020, in force since 1 Jan. 2021 ([AS 2020 6081](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2020/1009)).

#### Art. 61

**Transactions**[^61-1]

<sup>1</sup> Emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates and attestations are freely tradable.

<sup>2</sup> The authorised representatives for the account, authorised auction agents and the transaction validators and bid validators have the right to secure access to the Emissions Trading Registry.

<sup>3</sup>

When ordering a transaction involving emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations, authorised representatives for the account must give details of:

- a. the source and destination accounts; and
- b. type and quantity of emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations to be transferred.

<sup>4</sup> The emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations are transferred when the transaction validator consents to the transfer.

<sup>5</sup> The transaction is carried out according to a standardised procedure.

[^61-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).

#### Art. 62

**Registry management**[^62-1]

<sup>1</sup> The FOEN manages the Emissions Trading Registry electronically and records all transactions and auction bids.

<sup>2</sup> It ensures that it is possible to reproduce all the data relevant to transactions and auction bids at any time.

<sup>3</sup> In addition to the information submitted when an account is opened, it may also require further information at any time if necessary for the secure operation of the Registry.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN shall review whether the information transmitted for the account opening is still complete, current and correct at least once every three years and requires the account holder to report any changes if applicable.[^62-2]

[^62-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^62-2]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 63

**Exclusion of liability**

The Confederation accepts no liability for any losses incurred due to:

- a.[^63-1] errors in transactions involving emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates, attestations and auction bids;
- b. restricted access to the Emissions Trading Registry;
- c. misuse of the Emissions Trading Registry by third parties.

[^63-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).

#### Art. 64

**Account freezing and closure**[^64-1]

<sup>1</sup> If the Emissions Trading Registry regulations are contravened or if an investigation is pending due to an offence under Article 59*a* paragraph 1 letter b, then the FOEN freezes the user access or accounts concerned. The freeze lasts until such time as the regulations are adhered to or the investigation is concluded.

<sup>2</sup>

The FOEN may close accounts:

- a. that do not contain any emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates or attestations and that have not been used for at least a year;
- b. whose holder or registered user has contravened the Emissions Trading Registry regulations for at least a year;
- c. if the annual account management fees have not been paid for over a year.[^64-2]

<sup>2bis</sup> The FOEN shall from 1 January 2026 close the operator accounts of operators with a reduction obligation in accordance with Article 31 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act. The operators concerned may open a personal account in accordance with Article 57 paragraph 4.[^64-3]

<sup>3</sup> If an account to be closed has a positive balance, the FOEN shall request the account holder to designate another account within 40 working days to which the units are to be transferred. If this request is not met, the FOEN shall cancel the applicable units.[^64-4]

[^64-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 8 Oct. 2014, in force since 1 Dec. 2014 ([AS 2014 3293](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2014/573)).
[^64-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^64-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^64-4]: Inserted by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

#### Art. 65

**Publication of information and data protection**[^65-1]

The FOEN may, subject to preservation of manufacturing and trade secrecy, electronically publish the following data held in the Emissions Trading Registry:

- a. account number;
- b.

  for the following persons, contact details and data in accordance with proof of identity:

  - 1\. persons in accordance with Article 57 paragraphs 1–4,
  - 2\. bid validators,
  - 3\. authorised auction agents,
  - 4\. authorised representatives for the account,
  - 5\. transaction validators;

- c. emission allowances, emission-reduction certificates and attestations per account;
- c<sup>bis</sup>. transactions;
- d.[^65-2] for ETS participants: installation, aircraft and emissions data, the quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge, the quantity of emission allowances and emission-reduction certificates delivered to meet their obligation;
- d<sup>bis</sup>.[^65-3] for aircraft operators that were administered by a foreign authority before the ETS Agreement[^65-4] came into force: aircraft and emissions data, the quantity of emission allowances allocated free of charge, the quantity of emission allowances and emission reduction certificates surrendered to meet their obligation, in each case since 2012 at the earliest;
- d<sup>ter</sup>.[^65-5] for auctions: auction bids, auction date and quantity, minimum and maximum bid quantity, purchase price and purchased quantity, participants in the auction;
- e.[^65-6] for projects and programmes for domestic and foreign emission-reductions or increases in the carbon sink capacity: the quantity of attestations issued per monitoring period, the account number of the operator or personal accounts to which the attestations for the project or programme have been issued, and serial numbers of the attestations issued;
- f.[^65-7] for persons with offsetting obligations: the amount of the offsetting obligation and the quantity of attestations, emission-reduction certificates and emission allowances delivered to meet the obligation;
- g.[^65-8] for operators with reduction obligations: the quantity of emission-reduction certificates, emission allowances and attestations delivered to meet the obligation.

[^65-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, in force since 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).
[^65-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-3]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-4]: [SR 0.814.011.268](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/3477)
[^65-5]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-6]: Amended by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).
[^65-7]: Amended by No I of the O of 4 May 2022, in force since 1 June 2022 ([AS 2022 311](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2022/311)).
[^65-8]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

## Chapter 5 Commitment to Reduce Greenhouse Gas Emissions

### Section 1 Requirements and Content

[^chap5-sec1-1]

[^chap5-sec1-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 66

**Requirements**[^66-1]

<sup>1</sup> In accordance with Article 31 paragraph 1 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act, installation operators may enter into a reduction obligation if the greenhouse gas emissions generated by economic or public-sector activities constitute at least 60 per cent of the total greenhouse gas emissions generated at the site.

<sup>2</sup>

An economic activity is conducted if the installation operator:

- a. is registered in the commercial registry;
- b. has a business identification number (UID number); and
- c. conducts the activity in competition with other market players.

<sup>3</sup> The generation of heat or cooling for residential buildings is not considered an economic activity.

<sup>4</sup>

A reduction obligation may be entered into if installations operated by the local council are used for one of the following public activities:

- a. Operation of swimming pools;
- b. Operation of ice rinks;
- c. Operation of steam-powered locomotives and ships;
- d. Operation of hospitals, retirement homes and nursing homes;
- e. Production of fossil-based heat or cooling that is fed into regional district heating and cooling networks or used by installation operators as defined in paragraph 1; this does not include the production of heat or cooling for residential buildings.

<sup>5</sup> Installation operators that receive a grant for reducing carbon emissions in buildings may not enter into a reduction obligation for the given year.

[^66-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 66a

**Content of the reduction obligation**[^66a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

With the reduction obligation, the operator undertakes to:

- a. achieve an increase in its greenhouse gas efficiency that enables it to meet its greenhouse gas efficiency target derived from the target agreement under Article 41 or Article 46 paragraph 2 EnA[^66a-2], but which is at least 2.25 percent annually compared to the baseline value of the target agreement (reduction obligation with greenhouse gas efficiency target); or
- b. achieve an overall effect of its measures that complies with its measure target derived from the target agreement under Article 41 or Article 46 paragraph 2 EnA, but at least an overall effect that corresponds to an annual greenhouse gas reduction of at least 2.25 percent compared to the baseline value of the target agreement (reduction obligation with measure target).

<sup>1bis</sup> An operator may request the FOEN to approve a minimum value of less than 2.25 per cent if it can show in the decarbonisation plan that the greenhouse gas emissions are caused to a significant extent by the use of high-temperature process heat and that no reasonable alternative to the use of standard fossil fuels is available.[^66a-3]

<sup>2</sup> The minimum value of 2.25 per cent and any reduced minimum values approved in accordance with paragraph 1<sup>bis</sup> apply only to greenhouse gas emissions from standard fossil fuels.[^66a-4]

<sup>3</sup> The reduction obligation may also include measures to capture carbon and store it permanently in geological storage sites or chemically bind it permanently in mineral carbonates used in construction products, in accordance with the requirements of Annex 19.

<sup>4</sup> For the purpose of setting the greenhouse gas efficiency target or measure target, all measures with a payback period of up to six years shall be taken into account. For infrastructure measures, in particular measures relating to buildings, durable installations and installations involving the use of several products or processes, a payback period of up to twelve years shall apply.

[^66a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^66a-2]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8495)
[^66a-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).
[^66a-4]: Amended by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

#### Art. 67

**Reduction obligation with greenhouse gas efficiency target**[^67-1]

Operators of the following installations may enter into a reduction obligation with a greenhouse gas efficiency target:

- [tab] Operators whose facilities have emitted greenhouse gases amounting to at least 200 tonnes of CO<sub>2</sub>eq annually in the previous two years;
- b. Operators who wish to apply for a refund of the network surcharge in accordance with Article 39 EnA[^67-2].

[^67-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^67-2]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8495)

#### Art. 68

**Reduction obligation with greenhouse gas efficiency target**[^68-1]

Operators whose installations have emitted no more than 1,500 tonnes CO<sub>2</sub>eq per year over the previous two years may enter into a reduction obligation with a measures target.

[^68-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 68a

**Reduction obligation pool**[^68a-1]

<sup>1</sup> Installation operators may form a reduction obligation pool if the requirements under Article 66 are met for each individual site.

<sup>2</sup> The pool’s target agreement pursuant to Article 41 or Article 46 paragraph 2 EnA[^68a-2] must include all sites of the operators involved. A pool may consist of a maximum of 50 sites. The FOEN may grant exceptions upon request if the sites are centrally managed.

<sup>3</sup> The decarbonisation plan must specify the measures for each site. Several decarbonisation plans may be submitted per pool.

<sup>4</sup> The pool must designate a representative.

[^68a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^68a-2]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8495)

### Section 2 Application for the Determination of a Reduction Obligation

[^chap5-sec2-1]

[^chap5-sec2-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 69

[^69-1]

<sup>1</sup> An application to establish a reduction obligation must be submitted to the FOEN by 1 September of the previous year via the information and documentation system pursuant to Article 40*c* paragraph 1 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.

<sup>2</sup>

The application must include:

- a. the name and address of the installation operator;
- b. in the case of a pool, the names and addresses of all operators involved;
- c. the names and addresses of the representatives;
- d. information about the economic or public activity;
- e. the greenhouse gas emissions of the past two years in tonnes CO<sub>2</sub>eq;
- f. an analysis of the reduction potential;
- g. the federal building identifier (EGID) for each installation;
- h. the business identification number (UID);
- i. details of the applicable OASI compensation office and the OASI accounting numbers;
- j. if, in addition to the installations for which it is applying for the reduction obligation to be determined, an operator also operates installations for which it does not receive a CO<sub>2</sub> levy refund or with which it participates in the ETS: information on how these installations are kept separate in the operator’s OASI accounting numbers;
- k. the target agreement pursuant to Article 41 or Article 46 paragraph 2 EnA[^69-2] including the targeted greenhouse gas efficiency target or measures target.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN may request additional information if required in order to determine reduction obligations.

<sup>4</sup> If an installation operator uses fuels other than regular fossil fuels, the FOEN may request the installation operator to submit a monitoring plan in accordance with Article 51.

<sup>5</sup> If the information referred to in paragraph 2 letters f and k is not yet available at the time the application is submitted, the FOEN may, upon request, extend the deadline for submission of this information by a reasonable period.

[^69-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^69-2]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8495)

****Arts 70** and **71**[^7071-1]**

[^7071-1]: Repealed by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

### Section 3 Monitoring Report and Decarbonisation Plan

[^chap5-sec3-1]

[^chap5-sec3-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 72

**Monitoring report**[^72-1]

<sup>1</sup> Installation operators with a reduction obligation must submit to the FOEN a monitoring report in the form required by 31 May of each year.

<sup>2</sup>

The monitoring report must contain the following information from the previous year:

- a. information about the progression of greenhouse gas emissions;
- b. information about the measures taken and their effectiveness in reducing greenhouse gas emissions;
- c. in the case of a reduction obligation with greenhouse gas efficiency target: information about the progression of greenhouse gas efficiency;
- d. information about the progression of production indicators;
- e. an accounting of thermal fuels;
- f. information about possible deviations from the reduction pathway or measures target with a justification and planned corrective measures:
- g. information on the type and effect of the measures specified in the target agreement pursuant to Article 41 or Article 46 paragraph 2 EnA[^72-2] that cannot be counted toward fulfilment of the reduction obligation pursuant to Article 72*d*; and
- h. an overview in the form of a time series comparing the data for the monitoring year with the previous years’ data and the target values.

<sup>3</sup> If a measure involves the use of renewable fuels, the operator must show that the corresponding guarantees of origin have been assigned to them in the guarantees of origin system for thermal and motor fuels in accordance with Section 2*a* of the Energy Ordinance of 1 November 2017 (EnO)[^72-3]. If a guarantee of origin can be provided, the emission factor for these fuels is zero. The quantity of renewable fuels must be shown on the invoices.

<sup>4</sup> The FOEN may request additional information if required to assess monitoring.

[^72-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^72-2]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8495)
[^72-3]: [SR 730.01](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8496)

#### Art. 72a

**Content of the decarbonisation plan**[^72a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The decarbonisation plan in accordance with Article 31*a* letter b of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act must contain as a minimum:

- a.[^72a-2] accounting of all direct greenhouse gas emissions (Art. 2 let. b Federal Act of 30 September 2022 on Climate Protection Goals, Innovation and Strengthening Energy Security [CIA][^72a-3]) from fossil thermal fuels;
- b. a description of existing installations and processes;
- c. an analysis of which solutions can reduce greenhouse gas emissions from fossil fuels and to what extent;
- d. based on the analysis referred to under letter c, the measures to be taken to reduce greenhouse gas emissions from fossil thermal fuels; and
- e. a reduction pathway for direct greenhouse gas emissions up to 2040; the reduction path must be based on the net-zero target under Article 3 CIA and on the benchmarks set out in Article 4 CIA.

<sup>2</sup>

The following information must be given for the measures in paragraph 1 letter d:

- a. a precise description of the measures;
- b. an estimate of the costs of implementing the measures;
- c. a calculation of the effect to be achieved by the measures in tonnes of CO<sub>2</sub>eq and the associated impact on energy consumption;
- d. an implementation schedule.

<sup>3</sup> The submission of national or international attestations may not be claimed as a measure under the decarbonisation plan.

[^72a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^72a-2]: Amended by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).
[^72a-3]: [SR 814.310](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/40153)

#### Art. 72b

**Review of decarbonisation plan**[^72b-1]

The decarbonisation plan must be reviewed by a person who is registered in accordance with Article 8 of the Climate Protection Ordinance of 27 November 2024[^72b-2] or who works for a private organisation providing services under Article 39 paragraph 2 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.

[^72b-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^72b-2]: [SR 814.310.1](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/40265)

#### Art. 72c

**Submitting and updating the decarbonisation plan**[^72c-1]

<sup>1</sup> The decarbonisation plan must be submitted to the FOEN for the first time by 31 December of the third year of the reduction obligation.

<sup>2</sup> It must be updated every three years and submitted to the FOEN by 31 December of each year.

<sup>3</sup> The decarbonisation plan must be submitted and updated via the information and documentation system operated by the FOEN under Article 40*c* paragraph 1 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.

[^72c-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

### Section 4 Meeting the Reduction Obligation

[^chap5-sec4-1]

[^chap5-sec4-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 72d

**Non-crediting of emission reductions**

The following may not be counted towards meeting the reduction obligation:

- a. emission reductions for which attestations in accordance with Article 9 paragraph 7 have been issued;
- b. emission reductions which are attributable to measures for which federal financial assistance was granted.

#### Art. 72e

**Crediting of attestations to meet the reduction obligation in 2030**

<sup>1</sup> If an operator has not achieved the greenhouse gas efficiency or measures target specified in the reduction obligation in the period 2025–2030, they may, upon request, have national and international attestations amounting to 2.5 per cent of the greenhouse gas emissions for the years 2025–2030 credited towards meeting the reduction obligation.

<sup>2</sup> If the operator has only entered into a reduction obligation for part of the 2025–2030 period, the amount that may be credited under paragraph 1 shall be reduced pro rata temporis.

#### Art. 72f

**Non-crediting of additional greenhouse gas emissions when changing energy sources and in electricity production when reserves are activated**

<sup>1</sup>

If an operator’s installations emit more greenhouse gases for one of the following reasons, a request may be submitted not to take the additional greenhouse gas emissions into account when assessing compliance with the reduction obligation:

- a. Change of energy source due to an order by the Federal Council or a recommendation by the Federal Department of Economic Affairs, Education and Research (EAER) and DETEC;
- b. Electricity production as a result of an activation of reserves in accordance with the Winter Reserve Ordinance of 25 January 2023[^72f-1].

<sup>2</sup> The application for additional greenhouse gas emissions not to be credited must be submitted to the FOEN by 31 May of the following year in the form prescribed by the FOEN.

<sup>3</sup>

It must contain the following information:

- a. type and amount of the energy source replaced in the previous year and the new energy source in the case of a change of energy source or of the additional energy source used for electricity production;
- b. amount of greenhouse gas emissions generated in the previous year; and
- c. time during which the other or new energy source was used in the previous year or electricity was produced as a result of an activation of reserves.

[^72f-1]: [SR 734.722](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/40050)

### Section 5 Changes to and Early Termination of the Reduction Obligation

[^chap5-sec5-1]

[^chap5-sec5-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 73

**Duty to report changes**[^73-1]

Installation operators must notify the FOEN immediately of:

- a. any changes that could affect the reduction obligation;
- b. any changes that could affect the obligation to participate in the ETS;
- c. any change of installation operator;
- d. any change of OASI compensation office or accounting number;
- e. any change in the contact details of the representative.

[^73-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 73a

**Release from the reduction obligation of an operator in an emissions pool**[^73a-1]

<sup>1</sup>

An operator of installations may be released from the reduction obligation in an emissions pool for a site if:

- a. the installations are sold;
- b. the operator is required to take part in the ETS as a result of an increase in the installations’ greenhouse gas emissions;
- c. fossil thermal fuels are no longer used to generate energy in normal operation of the installations;
- d. it no longer meets the requirements under Article 66; or
- e. a target agreement under Article 31*b* paragraph 2 CO<sub>2</sub> Act no longer exists or it does not submit a decarbonisation plan.

<sup>2</sup> No reduction obligation may be entered into for a site for which an operator has been released from a reduction obligation.

[^73a-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 74

**Change in the emission reduction obligation**[^74-1]

<sup>1</sup>

The FOEN shall change the emissions target if a change is indicated for one of the following reasons in particular:

- a. The target values in the target agreement in accordance with Article 41 or Article 46 paragraph 2 EnA[^74-2] are amended.
- b. The target agreement in accordance with Article 41 or Article 46 paragraph 2 EnA is replaced by a new agreement.
- c. An operator is no longer bound by the reduction obligation (Art. 73*a* or 74*c*).
- d. The reduction obligation must be changed as the result of a notification under Article 73.

<sup>2</sup> If there is a change to the reduction obligation, the amended obligation applies retroactively from the beginning of the year in which the changed circumstances take effect.

<sup>3</sup> The FOEN shall also change a reduction obligation if the requirements in Article 66*a* paragraph 1<sup>bis</sup> are met. The changed obligation applies retroactively from a time determined by the FOEN.[^74-3]

[^74-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).
[^74-2]: [SR 730.0](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8495)
[^74-3]: Inserted by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

****Arts 74***a* and **74***b*[^74a74b-1]**

[^74a74b-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 1 Nov. 2017 ([AS 2017 6753](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2017/757)). Repealed by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 74c

**Premature termination of the reduction obligation**[^74c-1]

<sup>1</sup> Any operator that wishes to end its reduction obligation prematurely with effect from 31 December 2030 must apply to the FOEN to do so by 31 May 2031.

<sup>2</sup>

Any operator that wishes to end its reduction obligation prematurely with effect from the end of the calendar year for one of the following reasons must apply to the FOEN to do so by 31 May of the following year:

- a. obligation to take part in the ETS as a result of an increase in the installations’ greenhouse gas emissions; or
- b. fossil fuels are no longer used to generate energy for normal operations.

[^74c-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 75

[^75-1]

[^75-1]: Repealed by No I of the O of 19 Nov. 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2026 ([AS 2025 788](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/788)).

### Section 6 Failure to Meet the Reduction Obligation and Determining the Penalty

[^chap5-sec6-1]

[^chap5-sec6-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 76

**Failure to meet the reduction obligation**[^76-1]

<sup>1</sup> If an operator fails to meet its reduction obligation by not achieving the target values in 2030 or in 2040, the FOEN shall impose a penalty in accordance with Article 32 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.

<sup>2</sup> If the reduction obligation is not met, the amount of excess emitted tonnes CO<sub>2</sub>eq is calculated based on the degree to which the measures failed to deliver the expected impact.

<sup>3</sup> The payment deadline is 30 days from the notification of the ruling. If a payment is late, default interest is charged. The FDF shall fix the rate of interest.

[^76-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 77

**Security for the penalty**[^77-1]

If an installation operator is at risk of not meeting the target values, then the FOEN may require the Federal Office for Customs and Border Security (FOCBS) to provide security for the expected penalty in accordance with Article 32 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act until the risk no longer exists.

[^77-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 78

[^78-1]

[^78-1]: Repealed by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, with effect from 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

### Section 7 Publication of Information

[^chap5-sec7-1]

[^chap5-sec7-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 79

[^79-1]

The FOEN may, subject to the preservation of manufacturing and trade secrecy, publish information on installation operators with a reduction obligation, in particular:

- a. the names and addresses of installation operators;
- b. greenhouse gas efficiency targets and measures targets and whether these are achieved;
- c. reduction paths as published in the decarbonisation plans and whether these are achieved;
- d. the private organisations whose services are called upon under Article 39 paragraph 2 of the CO<sub>2</sub> Act.

[^79-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

## Chapter 6 …

****Arts 80**–**85**[^8085-1]**

[^8085-1]: Repealed by No I of the O of 13 Nov. 2019, with effect from 1 Jan. 2020 ([AS 2019 4335](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2019/753)).

## Chapter 7 Measures Relating to Fossil Motor Fuels

[^chap7-1]

[^chap7-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

### Section 1 Offsetting Carbon Emissions from Fossil Motor Fuels

[^chap7-sec1-1]

[^chap7-sec1-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 86

**Offsetting obligation**

<sup>1</sup>

Persons or installation operators are subject to offsetting obligations if:

- a. they release motor fuels for consumption pursuant Annex 10; or
- b. they convert fossil gases for combustion purposes to gases for use as motor fuels pursuant to Annex 10.

<sup>2</sup> Carbon emissions from motor fuels that, pursuant to Article 17 of the Mineral Oil Tax Act of 21 June 1996[^86-1], are entirely exempt from mineral oil tax, need not be offset.

[^86-1]: [SR 641.61](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/vocabulary/legal-taxonomy/8138)

#### Art. 87

**Exemptions from the offsetting obligation for small quantities**[^87-1]

<sup>1</sup> The obligations in Article 86 paragraph 1 do not apply to persons who, in the preceding three years, have released such small quantities of motor fuels for consumption that their use as a source of energy has resulted in emissions of less than 10,000 tonnes CO<sub>2</sub> per year.

<sup>2</sup> The exemption from the offsetting obligation extends until the beginning of the year in which the carbon emissions resulting from the use as a source of energy of motor fuels for consumption exceed 10,000 tonnes.

[^87-1]: Amended by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 Jan. 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 88

**Offsetting pools**

<sup>1</sup> Persons with offsetting obligations may apply to the FOEN each year by 30 November of the previous year to be treated as an offsetting pool.

<sup>2</sup> An offsetting pool has the rights and obligations of an individual person with offsetting obligations.

<sup>3</sup> It shall designate a representative.

<sup>4</sup> The representative’s headquarters shall be the sole delivery address.[^88-1]

[^88-1]: Inserted by No I of the O of 2 April 2025, in force since 1 May 2025 ([AS 2025 248](https://fedlex.data.admin.ch/eli/oc/2025/248)).

#### Art. 89

**Offsetting rate**[^89-1]

<sup>1</sup> Carbon emissions that result from the use of motor fuels as a source of energy released for consumption in the relevant year must be offset.
